Word omissions for the 66 Sol windows

171 accepted · 56 rejected · 66 windows with at least one accepted omission

Existing proposals by gpt-6.1-sol from sol_scratch66_omission_word_bank_auto_v4.json. Re-reviewed independently by gpt-6-astra; no new proposals. Each preview removes one highlighted span in isolation. Character counts exclude punctuation. Combining edits requires a new read of the window.

Accepted without flags: 108. Accepted with flags: 63 (included in the accepted total).

Word level refers to one source lexical item or information-bearing function word, not one Chinese character. Necessary grammatical padding may be included.

Word omissions must remove substantive passage content. Emphasis-only changes and ordinary ellipsis preserving the same facts are rejected. Concrete attributes can qualify when the reviewer identifies the specific detail lost.

Failed checks · information omitted (45) · valid omission unit (10) · valid in context (2) · fluent (1). One rejected candidate can fail more than one check.

Acceptance requires yes on all 5 checks — information omitted, fluent, deletion only, valid omission unit, valid in context. A no or uncertain answer causes rejection. Fluency requires natural, grammatical Chinese.

The omission treatment removes source information through deletion. Fluent omissions can be accepted with additional semantic distortion flags. These flags record secondary mistranslation effects without causing rejection. Possible other meaning changed records uncertainty about the secondary effect.

Flags on accepted omissions · recoverable from context (48) · other meaning changed (11) · ambiguous reference (6) · possible other meaning changed (4). A candidate can have several flags.

Re-reviewed 227 unchanged proposals from sol_scratch66_omission_word_bank_auto_v4.json. No new proposals were requested. Prior decisions were not shown to the reviewer.

  • Acts 20:10–12 · 又 (recoverable from context) — In this sequence, 又 marks the ascent as a return, aligning with “back.” 上楼 retains the ascent but omits its explicit return character. The earlier 下楼 makes that relationship inferable. This is a fluent deletion of one information-bearing adverb.
  • Acts 20:10–12 · 活着 (recoverable from context) — 活着 aligns with the single source adjective “alive” and specifies the youth's condition when brought home. The fluent remainder states only that he was brought home and the people were relieved. His living condition is inferable from Paul's statement in 20:10, but is no longer expressed in this verse.
  • Acts 23:12–15 · 多 (other meaning changed) — 多 expresses the source's quantity qualifier 'more than.' Its deletion loses the fact that participation exceeded forty. It also changes the retained numerical assertion from more than forty participants to forty participants.
  • Acts 23:12–15 · 更 — 更 marks the comparative degree in 'more carefully.' The edited pretext still promises careful examination, but no longer presents it as more careful than the comparison standard. This is a substantive comparative qualification, not merely ornamental emphasis.
  • Acts 23:16–18 · 于是 (recoverable from context) — 'Then' / 于是 explicitly places Paul's summons after the nephew's report. The deletion retains the summons but removes its explicit temporal link to that report. The sequence remains inferable from the adjacent verses. The single-connective deletion is fluent and otherwise unchanged.
  • Acts 23:16–18 · 年轻 (recoverable from context) — Deleting 年轻 removes the source adjective 'young': Paul's instruction now identifies 'this person' without describing his youth. The wording remains natural. Verse 23:18 independently retains the same age description, making the omitted property recoverable.
  • Acts 23:16–18 · 囚犯 — Deleting 囚犯 removes the aligned noun 'prisoner', so the centurion names Paul without stating his custodial status. The request and its participants remain intact, and the exact remainder is fluent.
  • Acts 23:19–22 · 年轻 (recoverable from context) — Deleting 年轻 removes the source's age property, 'young,' while leaving natural Chinese identifying the person whose hand the commander took. This is one adjective-level omission. His youth remains recoverable from 那年轻人 in 23:22.
  • Acts 23:19–22 · 明天 — Deleting 明天 removes the specified day for bringing Paul to the Sanhedrin. The remaining request is fluent and retains the action and destination but leaves its timing unspecified. 'Tomorrow' is one temporal lexical item.
  • Acts 23:19–22 · 多 (other meaning changed) — Deleting the quantitative qualifier 多 removes 'more than' and leaves fluent 四十人. It is a single semantic qualifier, not an arbitrary word fragment. The retained numerical assertion also changes from more than forty men to forty men, creating a secondary inaccurate count.
  • Acts 23:23–24 · 随后， — Deleting 随后， removes the explicit temporal sequencing conveyed by “Then.” The summons remains, but its placement after preceding events is no longer stated. This is a single temporal adverb with adjacent punctuation, and the remainder is natural Chinese.
  • Acts 23:23–24 · 两位 — Deleting 两位 removes the number “two”; 百夫长 alone does not specify how many centurions were summoned. 位 is classifier padding accompanying one numeral. The exact deletion leaves fluent Chinese without independently changing the summons.
  • Acts 23:23–24 · 安全 — Deleting 安全 removes the explicit requirement that Paul arrive safely. 护送 retains protective escort, but does not fully express safe arrival as a required outcome. The deletion targets one adverb and leaves the natural wording 护送他到达腓力斯总督那里.
  • Acts 23:23–24 · 总督 — Deleting 总督 removes Felix's title, aligned with “Governor.” The destination remains Felix, but his office is no longer identified anywhere in the displayed window. This single-title deletion leaves fluent Chinese.
  • Acts 23:25–26 · 总督 — Deleting “总督” removes Felix’s office as governor. “腓力斯阁下” remains a natural respectful address but does not specify that office. This is an aligned, single-title deletion.
  • Acts 23:31–35 · 继续 (recoverable from context) — English 'on' and Chinese 继续 mark continuation of the journey. The edited sentence says the horsemen escort Paul without specifying continuation.   • Acts 23:31–35 · 你的 (recoverable from context) — Deleting 你的 turns 控告你的人 into the natural legal noun 控告人. This removes the explicit identification of Paul as the person accused, aligned with English 'your.' The retained reference to 'your case' makes that relationship inferable. The deletion targets one possessive relation, with 的 serving as grammatical padding.
  • Acts 23:31–35 · 随后， (recoverable from context) — 随后 aligns with temporal 'Then': the custody order follows the governor's statement. The resulting Chinese is fluent, and the adjacent comma is permissible padding around one temporal adverb.

Rejected (56)
  • 1 Kings 13:1–3 · 必 — The selected 必 expresses certainty in the predicted splitting, corresponding to 'Surely.' Removing it weakens explicit assurance but leaves the same predicted event within the announced sign; it does not remove a substantive condition or event detail. The single-word deletion is mechanically exact and the remainder is fluent.
  • 1 Kings 13:6–7 · 我的 — The possessive is a word-level target, but 为我祷告，使手恢复原状 naturally retains the speaker as the hand’s owner through ordinary Chinese body-part possession ellipsis. The request still concerns restoring the king’s hand; no substantive ownership detail disappears.
  • 1 Kings 13:6–7 · 王的 — 手 in 手就恢复了 naturally refers back to the king’s 我的手 in the immediately preceding request. Omitting 王的 is fluent anaphoric ellipsis, not loss of the owner’s identity. The word-level deletion preserves the reported restoration of the king’s hand.
  • 1 Kings 13:8–10 · 也 — 也 corresponds to concessive 'still,' but deleting it loses no substantive condition or event detail. 你就是…我不会 remains a natural concessive construction: the refusal holds even under the hypothetical offer. Only explicit reinforcement is reduced.
  • 1 Kings 13:11–14 · 当天 (possible other meaning changed) — Deleting 当天 removes the explicit restriction to deeds done “that day,” leaving fluent Chinese. However, the aligned English deletion is the temporal noun phrase “that day,” not one lexical item, so it fails the word-level requirement. Removing this restriction may also broaden 一切事 from all that day’s deeds to all his deeds in Bethel, although context can still limit its scope.
  • 1 Kings 13:23–25 · 他的 — The deletion targets the single possessive 'his' and leaves fluent Chinese. However, immediately after 将他咬死, 尸体 naturally denotes the killed man's body through ordinary Chinese ellipsis. No substantive ownership or identity information disappears, so the alleged omission is not supported in this occurrence.
  • 1 Kings 13:26–30 · 我的 — In this direct lament, 兄弟 remains a speaker-relative address meaning 'brother'; ordinary Chinese omission of 我的 preserves the relationship conveyed by 'my brother.' The exact deletion is fluent and targets one possessive with its grammatical particle, but no substantive passage fact disappears.
  • 1 Kings 13:33–34 · 他的 — The possessive 他的 aligns with 'his', but 不肯离开恶道 naturally still refers to Jeroboam's own evil ways under the continuing subject 耶罗波安. Ordinary Chinese ellipsis preserves the substantive attribution, so no information is omitted.
  • 2 Chronicles 26:3–5 · 五十二年 — The edited verse naturally states that Uzziah reigned in Jerusalem but omits how long. However, 五十二年 deletes both the numeral 'fifty-two' and the substantive time unit 'years': a duration phrase, not a word-level unit.
  • 2 Chronicles 26:6–8 · 他的 — Although 他的 corresponds to the single possessive 'his', its deletion leaves ordinary Chinese possessive ellipsis. The preceding mention of Uzziah and subsequent 因为他 keep 名声 understood as Uzziah's fame, so no substantive participant relation disappears.
  • 2 Chronicles 26:6–8 · 极其 — 极其 aligns with the single adverb 'exceedingly', but here it only intensifies 强盛. Both versions retain his becoming powerful as the reason for his fame spreading; no substantive condition or concrete contrast is removed.
  • 2 Chronicles 26:11–15 · 极大的 — 极大的 aligns with 'tremendously,' but here it intensifies the help rather than supplying a distinct substantive condition or event detail. The edit still states that he received help until he became powerful and preserves the causal explanation of his fame. It is a fluent, word-level deletion, but emphasis alone does not meet the content-loss threshold.
  • 2 Kings 11:1–3 · 所有 — The deletion targets the aligned single quantifier 'all' and leaves fluent Chinese. However, 灭绝王室后裔 still expresses exterminating the royal descendants as a group; 灭绝 retains the exhaustive goal. Removing 所有 does not establish a substantive content loss here.

  • Acts 23:12–15 · 不喝 — The edited vow prohibits eating but no longer prohibits drinking. The exact deletion is fluent, but 不喝 removes the complete coordinated predication of abstaining from drinking, making this a predicate-level omission rather than a word-level omission.
  • Acts 23:12–15 · 郑重 — 郑重 aligns with 'solemn,' but here it emphasizes the seriousness of an already binding oath. 起誓 still conveys the same oath and its unchanged commitment not to eat until Paul is killed; no substantive passage detail is lost under the study's threshold.
  • Acts 23:16–18 · 但 — Deleting 但 removes the aligned adversative connective, but the displayed window establishes no concrete contrasting fact that disappears.   • Acts 23:25–26 · 以下 — “以下” corresponds to “following,” but “这封信：” still naturally introduces the letter immediately below. The demonstrative and colon preserve the reference locally; no substantive passage information disappears.
  • Acts 23:25–26 · 阁下 — “阁下” aligns with the honorific “His Excellency.” Its deletion reduces ceremonial deference, but Felix’s identity, office as governor, and status as the addressee remain explicit. The honorific supplies no separate substantive role or event detail here.
  • Judges 17:1–2 · 一千一百舍客勒 — The natural edited sentence says silver was taken but gives no amount. However, 一千一百舍客勒 removes both “eleven hundred” and the substantive measurement unit “shekels”: a quantity phrase, not one lexical item.
  • Judges 17:7–8 · 犹大的 (recoverable from context) — Deleting 犹大的 removes Bethlehem's explicit location 'in Judah,' leaving a fluent statement that the Levite came from Bethlehem. The deletion removes the whole locative qualification, not merely a lexical name, so it is phrase-level. Verse 17:8 independently supplies the same location, making the lost detail recoverable.
  • Judges 17:9–13 · 犹大的 — The edit removes Bethlehem’s location in Judah while retaining Bethlehem as the man's place of origin. However, 犹大的 corresponds to the complete locative qualification 'in Judah,' not just the name 'Judah'; this is a phrase-level omission.
  • Judges 18:1–2 · 他们的 — The possessive corresponds to English 'their,' but 但人从宗族中派出 naturally retains the reading that the Danites selected men from their own clans. Ordinary Chinese possessive ellipsis preserves the relationship, so no substantive information is lost.
  • Judges 18:5–6 · 请 — 请 corresponds to the single courtesy word “Please.” Its deletion leaves natural Chinese and preserves the request to inquire of God about the journey’s success. Only politeness changes; no substantive passage detail disappears.
  • Judges 18:7–8 · 而毫无戒备 — Deleting 而毫无戒备 removes the property 'unsuspecting,' which security and quietness do not preserve. The adjective and its coordinating conjunction constitute one eligible lexical removal. However, the exact ending 安然生活，平静。 leaves an awkward, clipped trailing adjective rather than an idiomatic description, so fluency fails.
  • Judges 18:9–10 · 非常 — 非常 corresponds to the single source intensifier 'very'. Removing it leaves grammatical Chinese and preserves the substantive assessment that the land is good. Only the strength of that assessment changes, so this does not meet the substantive-content threshold.
  • Judges 18:11–13 · 犹大的 — The fluent deletion genuinely removes Kiriath-jearim's location in Judah. However, 犹大的 corresponds to the complete source locative relation 'in Judah,' not merely the name 'Judah'; this is a phrase-level omission and fails the word-level requirement.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 他们的 — The possessive corresponds to English 'their', but 对弟兄说 naturally retains the subject-linked relationship through ordinary Chinese possessive ellipsis. The men still address their brothers; no substantive relationship disappears.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 现在 — Here 'Now' introduces the concluding exhortation after the inventory, rather than establishing a distinct time condition. Removing 现在 reduces the explicit transitional and immediate force, but retains the same instruction to consider what to do in the present situation.
  • Judges 18:22–23 · 这么 — 这么 corresponds to the degree emphasis in ‘such a company’ and is a single modifier. Its deletion is fluent and mechanical, but 一大群人 still conveys a large company, while 竟 retains the surprised tone. No substantive participant, event, or size distinction disappears.
  • Judges 18:29–31 · 整个 — 整个 corresponds to the single qualifier 'whole,' but its deletion does not remove temporal coverage here. The remaining time frame 上帝的殿在示罗的时期 together with 一直都在那里 still says the image remained there throughout that period.
  • Judges 9:1–2 · 请 — Deleting 请 removes the politeness conveyed by “Please,” but retains the directive to ask all Shechem’s leaders and every substantive passage detail. The exact remainder is natural Chinese, and the deletion targets one aligned lexical item.
  • Judges 9:9–10 · 却 — Removing 却, aligned with “But,” removes adversative framing, not a substantive passage fact established in this window. The olive tree still gives exactly the same reply. The deletion targets one connective and leaves natural Chinese.
  • Judges 9:9–10 · 我们的 — In the trees’ direct invitation, 请来作王 naturally means to come and be their king. Chinese ordinarily leaves this locally supplied relationship implicit, so removing 我们的 does not substantively remove the intended subjects of the reign. The span corresponds to the single pronoun “us,” with 的 as grammatical padding.
  • Judges 9:13–14 · 却 — 却 corresponds to “But” and marks the reply as contrastive. Removing this single discourse marker leaves natural Chinese and preserves the speaker, reply, and all its substantive content. The lost rhetorical contrast does not meet the substantive-content threshold.
  • Judges 9:15–17 · 黎巴嫩的 — The edited verse retains the threatened burning of cedars but loses their geographic identification with Lebanon. It is fluent and deletion-only. However, 黎巴嫩的 removes the entire geographic modifier ‘of Lebanon,’ including its relation to the cedars, rather than just a lexical item; this is phrase-level.
  • Judges 9:21–25 · 流血的 — 流血的 aligns with the source's bloodshed imagery, but deleting it does not remove a substantive fact here. 这罪 still refers directly to the explicitly stated crime of murdering Jerubbaal's seventy sons. Responsibility for that bloodshed remains expressed. The deletion is lexical, and the exact remainder is fluent.
  • Judges 9:36–37 · 却 — 却 corresponds to the single adversative marker “But.” Its deletion is fluent and removes explicit contrast marking, but Zebul still replies by contradicting Gaal’s interpretation. No substantive passage fact disappears.
  • Judges 9:42–45 · 随后， — In this uninterrupted narrative, the demolition sentence still continues the sequence after the completed capture and killing. Removing 随后， removes an overt temporal transition, but ordinary Chinese narrative linkage preserves the same event order here. The deletion is exact and fluent, and targets one temporal adverb with adjacent punctuation; it fails the substantive-information-loss requirement.
  • Judges 9:46–49 · 也 — 也 aligns with 'also' and is a single additive adverb, but its removal does not remove a substantive passage fact. 每个人都砍了一根树枝 still states that every man cut a branch; Abimelech's preceding action remains explicit. Only the overt additive connection disappears, not an event, participant, or action property.
  • Judges 9:50–53 · 所有的 — Although 所有的 corresponds to the single quantifier “all,” the retained 都 still expresses total participation in 城中男女和首领都逃进那里. The deletion is mechanically exact and fluent, but the alleged loss of universal scope does not occur in this context.

安然 · 2 characters

18:7 · Accepted

So the five men departed and came to Laish, where they saw that the people were living securely, like the Sidonians, quiet and unsuspecting. There was nothing lacking in the land and no oppressive ruler. And they were far away from the Sidonians and had no alliance with anyone.

这五个人就离开那里，来到拉亿，见那里的居民像西顿人一样安然生活，平静而毫无戒备。那地一无所缺，也没有欺压人的统治者。他们离西顿人很远，也没有与任何人结盟。

After　这五个人就离开那里，来到拉亿，见那里的居民像西顿人一样生活，平静而毫无戒备。那地一无所缺，也没有欺压人的统治者。他们离西顿人很远，也没有与任何人结盟。
